CMPXCHG16B (M128) - Latency

Operands

  • Operand 1 (r/w): Memory
  • Operand 2 (r/w, suppressed): Register (RDX)
  • Operand 3 (r/w, suppressed): Register (RAX)
  • Operand 4 (r, suppressed): Register (RCX)
  • Operand 5 (r, suppressed): Register (RBX)
  • Operand 6 (w, suppressed): Flags (ZF: w)

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, base register): ≤20

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, index register): ≤20

Latency operand 1 → 1 (memory): 16

Latency operand 1 → 2 (address, base register): 14

Latency operand 1 → 2 (address, index register): 15

Latency operand 1 → 2 (memory): ≤24

Latency operand 1 → 3 (address, base register): 14

Latency operand 1 → 3 (address, index register): 14

Latency operand 1 → 3 (memory): ≤24

Latency operand 1 → 6 (address, base register): 18

Latency operand 1 → 6 (address, index register): 14

Latency operand 2 → 1: ≤11

Latency operand 2 → 2: 6

Latency operand 2 → 3: 6

Latency operand 2 → 6: 6

Latency operand 3 → 1: ≤11

Latency operand 3 → 2: 6

Latency operand 3 → 3: 6

Latency operand 3 → 6: 6

Latency operand 4 → 1: ≤7

Latency operand 4 → 2: 1

Latency operand 4 → 3: 0

Latency operand 4 → 6: 0

Latency operand 5 → 1: ≤7

Latency operand 5 → 2: 0

Latency operand 5 → 3: 0

Latency operand 5 → 6: 0

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, base register): ≤20
